Defensive hardening mapped to MITRE ATT&CK mitigations for T1222 File and Directory Permissions Modification and T1548 Abuse Elevation Control Mechanism. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/linux/linux-hardening-baseline/sub/linux-hardening-baseline-filesystem-permissions))
- **linux-hardening-baseline-kernel-parameters**: Use when tuning Linux kernel parameters with sysctl as part of a defensive hardening baseline. Defensive hardening mapped to MITRE ATT&CK mitigations for T1068 Exploitation for Privilege Escalation. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/linux/linux-hardening-baseline/sub/linux-hardening-baseline-kernel-parameters))
- **linux-hardening-baseline-ssh-hardening**: Use when hardening OpenSSH on a Linux server or auditing an existing sshd configuration. Defensive hardening mapped to MITRE ATT&CK mitigations for T1110 Brute Force and T1078 Valid Accounts. ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/linux/linux-hardening-baseline/sub/linux-hardening-baseline-ssh-hardening))
